The preceding hidden fire emerges in 100:2, and the later `شَهِيد` in 100:7 makes visibility evidentiary. Fire, morning opening, and witness form an increasing-visibility sequence. I infer that light makes the action inspectable; that directional relation is not grammatically asserted. `صُبْحًا` therefore reads as the threshold at which concealed motion becomes manifest and answerable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3e2fac8df686822f857b","text":"100:10 shifted the morning raid from external event to state-change of the chest: صبح B010 \"becoming\" now echoes the later transformation of hidden contents into account. The water/provision branches gained force only after 100:11's low-water خبير and ربّ water/cloud branches appeared.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001119/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_422ed64a0b1bd7cfc6f5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:3:1\",\"root_001119\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Durum değiştirme anlamı, baskının bozuk bir düzeni daha iyisiyle değiştirmeye yönelmiş olabileceği ihtimalini de açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000839/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_45deb88fec480d6e7138","text":"1. **A timed incursion that changes the state of a group.** `ص ب ح B004` explicitly supplies the morning raid and its alarm, while `غ ي ر B003` supplies alteration or substitution. The ayah stands between generated fire and the later raising of dust and entry into a `جَمْع`. Morning is the timing condition; incursion is the operation; changed collective order is the inferred result. The packet supplies raid, change, dust, and penetration, but I supply the arrow from incursion to altered state. The line reads as a transformative intervention at dawn rather than only arrival at a clock-time.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000839/B004","root_001119/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_569753546d7283c61c1f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:3:1\",\"root_001119\",[\"B003\"]],[\"100:3:2\",\"root_000839\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sabah baskını, yalnız zaman bildirmez; topluluğun düzenini zorla başka bir duruma geçirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000839/B001","root_000839/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5b2f56980c8f9dadbe63","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:3:2\",\"root_000839\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Şafak, önceki gizli ateşten sonraki eylemi görünür ve tanıklığa açık kılan bir eşiktir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000839/B003","root_001119/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_663be03efc1668ab47d5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:3:1\",\"root_001119\",[\"B001\"]],[\"100:3:2\",\"root_000839\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Sulama ve sabah içeceği çağrışımları, baskını ihtiyaç giderme arayışının şiddetli el koymaya dönüşmesi olarak da gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B002","root_000993/B003","root_001119/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_88848aa99e6bef17d699","text":"2. **Jealous/protective incursion.** Evidence: غ ي ر B004 protective jealousy over family; ع د و B003 enmity; ج م ع B002 assembled army/group; ر ح م B002 kinship bond. Structural cue: the raid language is placed before a moral claim about ingratitude toward the Lord. Role: ghirah supplies a protective social motive; جمع supplies a threatened or targeted collective. Abductive move: aggressive entry can be construed as protecting one bond by attacking another group. Reading change: the ayah preserves a possible honor/kinship mechanism alongside raw aggression.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001119/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a0a56db10c2bb90f0ee6","text":"100:6-8 retrospectively change the scale of `غ ي ر B003`: `ك ن د B002` supplies a disordered relation and `خ ي ر B001` supplies the desired evaluative opposite. This activates corrective substitution as a real coexisting possibility, but does not prove that the incursion's agents perform that correction. Meanwhile 100:7 confirms that dawn's increase in visibility has an evidentiary endpoint.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001119/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d8cd4533490a1c4a23a0","text":"3. **Exploratory corrective substitution.** The scope of `غ ي ر B003` includes displacing wrong with right, while 100:6 supplies `ك ن د B002` ingratitude and 100:8 supplies `خ ي ر B001` desired good. These later evaluative poles can activate a corrective, rather than merely destructive, function in the incursion. The packet does not say that the raiders target ingratitude; I infer that possibility from the ordered shift from penetration to the human moral diagnosis. On this coexisting reading, the dawn change can be an attempted replacement of a disordered state by a better one.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000839/B004","root_001119/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ef416f2a187fad70d0e9","text":"1. **Morning raid as transformation of state.** Evidence: غ ي ر B003 altering/substituting; ص ب ح B004 morning raid/alarm, B001 dawn opening, B010 becoming. Structural cue: after breath and sparks, dawn names both time and change of condition. Role: غير converts motion into altered social reality; صبح supplies the vulnerable opening of day. Abductive move: the attack's essence is not travel but changing an order before it has stabilized. Reading change: the ayah reads as a forced becoming: the morning turns into a raid-condition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000839/B003","root_001119/B001","root_001544/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fcce1b0eaacc562b48ce","text":"3. **Provisioning turned violent.** Evidence: غ ي ر B001 provision/watering/repair; ص ب ح B003 morning drink/watering; ق د ح B005 drawing from pot/well; ن ق ع B002 quenching thirst. Structural cue: morning and water/food branches cluster, yet the surface sequence is martial. Role: these branches recode the raid as a perverted procurement economy. Abductive move: need for provision may be the causal background that turns morning sustenance into violent taking. Reading change: the ayah can be read as a resource raid, where the imagery of breakfast/watering is inverted into seizure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
